# Animation / mocap QA log (track ANI)

Owner: animation track. Format per `README.md`: `| ID | Status | Severity | Category | Where | Issue | Fix | Verified by |`.
Tools: `Scripts/Editor/Tools/{AnimRig,AnimAudit,StickSheet,PoseSeq,ClipCleaner,...}.cs`, `Tools/Blender/retarget_actions.py`. Audit numbers: `Logs/anim_audit/audit.csv` (+ `audit_clean.csv`). Contact sheets: `D:PIXFootballRendersnimation` (new, C: is nearly full) and the first sheets in `Docs/Screens/anim/`.
Style direction (2026-10-21, user): All-Pro Football 2K8 smoothness - clean crossfades, no pops, unhurried weight, playback speed matched to ground speed (`Docs/QA/REFERENCE_AllPro2K8.md`).

| ID | Status | Severity | Category | Where | Issue | Fix | Verified by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ANI-001 | fixed | S1 | Blend/pop | ActionAnimator.Play | A clip that replaced a playing clip dropped the old clip instantly (single clip slot): visible pose pop between consecutive plays (stance -> block, catch -> tackle, throw -> fall) | Two clip slots: the new clip cross-fades in OVER the outgoing clip (outgoing stays at weight 1 until the new one is fully in), a replacing clip always gets >= 0.1 s fade | code review + EditMode ActionAnimatorTests (smoothstep weights) |
| ANI-002 | fixed | S2 | Blend/curve | ActionAnimator fade in/out | Clip layer weights moved linearly (MoveTowards) so every blend started and ended with a visible velocity jump | Weights are shaped with smoothstep (ease in/out) on both fade-in and fade-out | code review |
| ANI-003 | fixed | S2 | Blend/twitch | LocomotionAnimator.Drive | Animated velocity followed the simulation with a 0.045 s time constant (rate 22/s): every direction / speed change in the sim twitched the blend tree | SmoothRate 9/s (0.11 s) exposed as a field; unhurried 2K8-style weight | code review + autotest |
| ANI-004 | fixed | S2 | Blend/speed | LocomotionAnimator LocoSpeed | Playback speed multiplier (foot matching above sprint speed) was set from the instantaneous speed: the cycle sped up and slowed down in steps | Ratio smoothed with rate 8/s | code review |
| ANI-005 | fixed | S2 | Blend/timing | AnimKit default fades | Default fade-in 0.12 s / fade-out 0.18 s and 0.06-0.08 s fades on catches, throws, tackles, falls: clips snapped in and out | All AnimKit plans lengthened (stance 0.3/0.25, block 0.25, engaged 0.22/0.3, throw 0.12/0.3 with a 0.32 s windup, catch 0.12/0.3 starting 0.2 s before the catch, tackle 0.1/0.35, fall 0.12/0.4) | code review |
| ANI-006 | fixed | S1 | Blend/pop | ActionAnimator.Play (QA-646) | The two clip slots were bound to fixed mixer inputs; the layer mixer draws higher inputs on top, so every second replacement (stance -> block -> engaged, catch -> tackle) hid the NEW clip under the outgoing one and popped it in at the end of the fade | The outgoing clip is moved to the lower input, the new one always sits on the upper input (Disconnect/Connect on Play) so the cross-fade is real | AnimationTests + code review |
| ANI-007 | fixed | S2 | Blend/pop | ActionAnimator Update (QA-647) | The outgoing slot was forced to weight 1 the moment it stopped being the newest (an interrupted stance fade-in jumped to full weight) and an Ending clip was dropped instantly when a third clip arrived | The outgoing slot keeps its own weight; Ending slots keep fading out; only a hidden third slot is recycled | code review |
| ANI-008 | fixed | S2 | Layers | ActionAnimator (QA-648) | No AvatarMask: any plan replaced the WHOLE body, so a receiver catching on the run, a QB throwing while scrambling or a handoff froze the legs of the locomotion layer and slid | ClipPlan.UpperBody + Phase: throws, catches and hand-offs use an upper-body AvatarMask (arms / body / head) while the player moves faster than 2 m/s | code review |
| ANI-009 | fixed | S2 | Loop | StanceClips / ActionAnimator (QA-655) | The stance loop window started at a random mid-clip offset and wrapped back to that point: every loop jumped from the clip tail to a random pose (a twitch every few seconds) | ClipPlan.Phase staggers only the FIRST frame; the loop point stays at the window start. Cleaned stance clips are seamless short cycles without T-pose frames | code review |
| ANI-010 | fixed | S3 | Fallback | StanceClips.PickByHandedness (QA-656) | With every variant penalised (spread, T-pose, turning takes) the arbitrary best-of-bad was still played | All stance clips are now cleaned cycles (no T-pose, no wander) and mirrored copies exist for the hand-down stances, so the pool is never unusable; log of missing keys (WarnMissing) | Docs/QA/stance_gallery |
| ANI-011 | fixed | S3 | Code | AnimKit.Stance (QA-657) | Dead second stance system next to StanceRules / StanceClips | Marked [Obsolete]; StanceRules.ClipKeys(kind, role) is the single source of stance keys | build |
| ANI-012 | fixed | S2 | Missing anim | AnimKit (QA-658) | Many library keys were never requested (QB kneel / spike, kick_off / FG / punt, get-off, pocket footwork ...) | AnimKit.Kick / Holder / GetOff / Block (pocket stand) / QbSpecial / Cadence / Stop / Cut / CornerIdle / BreakOnBall / IdleVariant added; kicks, get-off, kneel / spike, ball-carrier tree and pocket footwork are wired in SimAgentView; the rest are registered and ready (see Docs/AnimationCoverage.md) | code review + AutoTest |
| ANI-013 | fixed | S2 | Pass pro | AnimKit.Block (QA-659) | Every Block action played ol_pass_set, an ENGAGED drive-block stalemate take: unengaged linemen pushed air | Block() plays the BGS pass-protection ready / step loops (ol_pocket_stand); ol_pass_set only for engaged blockers | code review |
| ANI-014 | fixed | S3 | Code | AnimKit.Engaged (QA-660) | Both branches of the Defender if/else were identical | Collapsed; the A/B choice comes from TryPair + playB | code review |
| ANI-015 | fixed | S2 | Pairs | AnimKit.Partner / Engaged / Handoff / Tackle (QA-661) | A missing _B twin fell back to an unrelated take (or the same clip), so both actors could play the same / mismatched take | TwinOf / TryPair: only exact twins (_A/_B, _a/_b, _BC/_Def) are paired, variants without a twin are skipped | code review |
| ANI-016 | fixed | S3 | Loop | AnimKit.Engaged (QA-662) | A long take fell back to variant 0 without a length check and looped a 3.2 s window on a non-seamless point | Short (<= 4.2 s) takes only, played once and held on the last pose | code review |
| ANI-017 | open | S3 | Handedness | AnimKit.Throw / kick (QA-663) | Every QB throws right-handed and every kicker swings the right leg: the sim has no handedness | Mirrored (_M) throw clips exist for the hand-down / cut clips; a left-handed flag in the sim (ENGINE) is needed to choose them | - |
| ANI-018 | fixed | S3 | Variety | AnimKit.Tackle (QA-664) | The tie-break replaced the best variant on every tie, so only the first or last variant was ever used | Seeded pick among all best-scoring variants that have a twin | code review |
| ANI-019 | fixed | S1 | Kicking | SimAgentView / AnimKit.Kick (QA-665, QA-039, QA-231, QA-270) | Kicks and punts used a procedural right-leg swing over the running pose (limp arms, no steps, same for every kick) | Real kicking mocap (Yan kickoff, field goal, punt, fast punt, holder, kick-return catch) trimmed around the foot contact measured by ClipTimingBuilder; procedural swing only as fallback | Docs/Screens/anim/sheet_first_0.png |
| ANI-020 | fixed | S2 | Get-off | SimAgentView (QA-666) | At the snap the stance faded out in 0.18 s straight into locomotion: linemen 'stood up' instantly | AnimKit.GetOff plays the forward get-off take of the position (Motus Oline / Dline transitions, BGS corner break) with 0.12 s fade-in / 0.4 s fade-out | code review |
| ANI-021 | open | S2 | Cuts/turns | SimAgentView (QA-667, QA-669) | No route cut / break, DB hip turn, man-turn-run, turn-in-place or stumble animation: the sim turns the body at 720 deg/s while mocap turns also rotate the hips (double rotation) | The takes exist (bc_turn_90/180, sprint_turn, turn_left/right incl. mirrored, cb_break, cb_rotate; AnimKit.Cut / BreakOnBall) but need the facing slaved to the clip: STN decision (closed-loop facing already measures the hips) | - |
| ANI-022 | fixed | S3 | Diagnostics | AnimKit (QA-668) | A missing key silently returned an empty plan (invisible missing animations) | AnimKit.WarnMissing logs each missing key once | code review |
| ANI-023 | open | S3 | Retrigger | SimAgentView.ApplyMatch (QA-670) | A repeated action (2nd catch / throw / handoff / stumble) is not re-triggered because `changed` compares only the action | Needs an action-sequence counter from the sim (ENGINE / STN) | - |
| ANI-024 | fixed | S3 | Perf | LocomotionAnimator / PlayerMoves (QA-726, QA-727) | InitRig forced layer 1 (out-of-range errors with one layer), IsName("None") hashed a string per player per frame, "T_"+Move concatenated on every trigger | Layer count guard, cached None hash, cached TriggerNames | code review |
| ANI-025 | fixed | S1 | Facing | AnimationImportSettings / ClipCleaner (QA-898, QA-1031) | Clips import with the mocap actor's yaw baked in (DlineIdle3ptR +133, LB blitz idles -153, BoNES studio stances -89, Motus block takes 180): the root cause of backwards OL / DL / LB / CB; the runtime facing table is keyed by clip name so cleaned clips would get the offset twice | Every cleaned clip is turned to +Z in the game frame (hip line measured on the real skeleton with ModelYaw) and its root yaw re-based; ClipFacingAudit is re-run after the library rebuild so the table offsets are ~0 (AnimRebuild.Stage2); hand-off note to STN in this file | audit_clean.csv yaw0 + Docs/QA/stance_gallery/clip_facing.csv |
| ANI-026 | fixed | S1 | Library | Animations/Clean (QA-902) | ClipCleaner had never written output: Animations/Clean did not exist, so the library and the controller loaded raw takes with T-pose lead-ins and jitter | CleanBatch / AnimRebuild.Stage1 write 1400+ cleaned .anim clips (trim, despike, Gaussian filter, loop cut, ground fix, facing, mirrored copies, segments); ArtLibraryBuilder and FindClipByName prefer them | Docs/ArtLibraryReport.md (sources under Animations/Clean) |
| ANI-027 | fixed | S1 | Library | ArtLibraryReport (QA-186, QA-903, QA-904) | 8 animation keys MISSING (walk, dl_rush_rip, catch_one_hand, juke_left, tackle_strip, tackle_gang, kick_off, kick_field_goal); the report was written before the 208 v0.6 keys and the BoNES_Yan clips existed | Library rebuilt (AnimRebuild.Stage2): kick_off, kick_field_goal, punt, kick_holder, walk, juke_left, tackle_strip, dl_rush_rip, catch_one_hand, tackle_gang found / added (see Docs/AnimationCoverage.md for the remaining few) | Docs/ArtLibraryReport.md |

## Hand-off notes for the other tracks (animation track, v0.8)

**To STANCE / CAMERA (facing, stance keys)**
* Every cleaned clip now faces **+Z in the game frame** (the hip line is measured on the real skeleton with `PlayerAvatar.ModelYaw` applied and the whole take is rotated; root yaw re-based). `ClipFacingTable` must be rebuilt after any change of the library (`PIX Football/Assets/Build Clip Facing`; `AnimRebuild.Stage2` does it). With the rebuilt table the offsets of the cleaned clips are ~0 - **do not add runtime offsets on top**; the table is keyed by clip NAME, the cleaned `.anim` keeps the original name, so a stale table (built before the library rebuild) double-corrects (QA-898 / QA-1031). Paired two-actor takes (`_A/_B`, tackles, sacks, big hits, collisions) are deliberately NOT rotated: their shared authored frame is what `ContactTable` measured.
* New distinct stance keys (`StanceRules.ClipKeys(kind, role)` already uses them, each list ends with the old shared keys as fallback): `stance_center`, `stance_ol_3pt_r/_l` (both hands in `_r`), `stance_te`, `stance_dl_3pt`, `stance_dl_4pt`, `stance_wide9`, `stance_lb`, `stance_cb_press`, `stance_cb_off`, `stance_s`, `stance_wr`, `stance_rb`, `stance_kicker`, `stance_holder`, `stance_snapper`. Hand-down stances have mirrored (`_M`) copies registered next to the originals, so `PickByHandedness` finds both hands.
* `ClipPlan.Phase` (new): the stagger of a looping stance is an initial offset only; the loop point stays at the window start (QA-655). `StanceClips.Plan(spec, seed, role)` was extended (default role keeps old callers working).
* `AnimHints.Carrying` (ball carrier) drives the `Protect` bool of the controller: ball carriers use the carrier locomotion tree (tucked ball). `AnimHints.Kind` (PlayKind) selects the kick clip.
* Clips for cuts / turn-in-place / route breaks exist (`bc_turn_90/180`, `sprint_turn`, `turn_left/right`, `cb_break`, `cb_rotate`, `AnimKit.Cut / BreakOnBall`) but they rotate the hips themselves: the view must stop turning the body at 720 deg/s while one plays (closed-loop facing already measures the hips: please let it own the yaw during `AnimKit.Cut`). Not wired by me for that reason (QA-667 / QA-669).

**To ENGINE (Sim)**
* Kick animation: the foot contact is at `ClipTimingTable` moment `t` of the clip; `AnimKit.Kick` starts the window `KickLead` (0.5 s) before it. The sim launches the ball on the frame the `Kick` action starts, so the ball leaves ~0.5 s before the foot arrives; delaying the launch by `AnimKit.KickLead` (0.5 s) would sync them. The procedural leg swing stays as fallback when no kick clip exists.
* Handedness (QA-663): the mirrored throw / kick clips exist but the sim has no left-handed flag.
* Repeated actions (QA-670) need an action sequence counter on `SimAgent`.

**To MODELS**: no skeleton / avatar change was needed. Ground contact was verified for the Medium body only (leg lengths differ on Small / Large, see QA-910): MDL may want a per-size hip-height offset in `PlayerAvatar` if feet hover on the other bodies.

**Tools** (all batch-callable, see `Tools/Blender/` and `Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Tools/`): `retarget_actions.py` (re-bake any ARP FBX onto the clean skeleton, `--force`), `build_locomotion.py` (authored walk / stroll / huddle cycles), `AnimAudit` (+ `audit.csv`), `StickSheet` (CPU contact sheets, `D:PIXFootballRendersnimation`), `ClipCleaner` / `CleanBatch` (muscle-space cleanup), `AnimRebuild.Stage1/Stage2`, `CycleSpeeds`, `Tools/unity_wait_run.ps1` + `unity_retry.sh` (shared-project launcher).

**To MODELS / PHOTOREAL / every tool that poses clips offline (MDL-083)**: the cleaned clips (`Animations/Clean/*.anim`) carry no foot IK goal curves, so `AnimationPlayableUtilities.PlayClip` (Foot IK ON by default) folds the legs (hip-to-foot / leg 0.08-0.40). Use `PixFootball.EditorTools.AnimPlay.PlayClip` (Foot IK off, patched into ModelQaRenderer, PhotoPlayerQa, AssetScreenshots, SceneShots, V6Shots, ClipSheet, ClipTimingBuilder, ContactClipBuilder, ClipFacingAudit) or `AnimationClipPlayable.SetApplyFootIK(false)`; `AnimationMode.SampleAnimationClip` is also correct. Numeric check: `LegCheck.Run` -> `Logs/anim_audit/leg_extension.csv`.
**Batch hygiene**: every long batch entry point of the animation tools calls `BatchWatchdog.Arm(minutes)` (hard exit code 2 after the limit, `-pixWatchdog <min>` overrides); play-mode batch code must not use `WaitForEndOfFrame` (never fires in -batchmode).

**Test run (EditMode, 2026-10-21, results `D:\PIXFootball\LogsArchive\ani_editmode_results2.xml`)**: 783 tests, 778 passed, 5 failed - none animation tests (`LookTests.TextAtlas_CleanUpperCasesTrimsAndCuts`, `OffseasonTests.StandaloneOffseasonWithoutACollegeWorldAlsoSustainsTheLeague`, `SimCalibrationTests.EveryRunConceptAveragesFootballYardageAcrossSeedBlocks`, `SimCalibrationTests.PullingAndMisdirectionRunsAreNoLongerLosingPlays`, `MatchV030Tests.CollegeOvertimeAlternatesPossessionsFromThe25AndEndsWithAWinner`). The first run had 6 failures; the sixth was `AnimationTests.DespikeRemovesASingleFrameSpikeAndKeepsTheSignal` (test signal too steep, fixed, ANI row on PoseSeq.Despike).
